					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 7:21} % *ECCLESIASTES-7-21
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-7-21}

			Also take no heed unto all words that are spoken; lest thou hear thy servant curse thee:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גַּ֤ם לְכָל־הַדְּבָרִים֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר יְדַבֵּ֔רוּ אַל־תִּתֵּ֖ן לִבֶּ֑ךָ אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹֽא־תִשְׁמַ֥ע אֶֽת־עַבְדְּךָ֖ מְקַלְלֶֽךָ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὅτι ἄνθρωπος οὐκ ἔστιν δίκαιος ἐν τῇ γῇ, ὃς ποιήσει ἀγαθὸν καὶ οὐχ ἁμαρτήσεται.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Also take not heed unto all words that are spoken, lest thou hear thy servant curse thee;
					\item[NIV] Do not pay attention to every word people say, or you may hear your servant cursing you—
					\item[NRSV] Do not give heed to everything that people say, or you may hear your servant cursing you;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 7:22} % *ECCLESIASTES-7-22
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-7-22}

			For oftentimes also thine own heart knoweth that thou thyself likewise hast cursed others.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֛י גַּם־פְּעָמִ֥ים רַבּ֖וֹת יָדַ֣ע לִבֶּ֑ךָ אֲשֶׁ֥ר אַתָּ֖ה קִלַּ֥לְתָּ אֲחֵרִֽים׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καί γε εἰς πάντας λόγους οὓς λαλήσουσιν ἀσεβεῖς μὴ θῇς καρδίαν σου, ὅπως μὴ ἀκούσῃς τοῦ δούλου σου καταρωμένου σε·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] for oftentimes also thine own heart knoweth that thou thyself likewise hast cursed others.
					\item[NIV] for you know in your heart that many times you yourself have cursed others.
					\item[NRSV] your heart knows that many times you have yourself cursed others.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 7:23} % *ECCLESIASTES-7-23
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-7-23}

			All this have I proved by wisdom: I said, I will be wise; but it was far from me.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כָּל־זֹ֖ה נִסִּ֣יתִי בַֽחָכְמָ֑ה אָמַ֣רְתִּי אֶחְכָּ֔מָה וְהִ֖יא רְחוֹקָ֥ה מִמֶּֽנִּי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὅτι πλειστάκις πονηρεύσεταί σε καὶ καθόδους πολλὰς κακώσει καρδία σου, ὅτι ὡς καί γε σὺ κατηράσω ἑτέρους.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] All this have I proved in wisdom: I said, I will be wise; but it was far from me.
					\item[NIV] All this I tested by wisdom and I said, “I am determined to be wise"— but this was beyond me.
					\item[NRSV] All this I have tested by wisdom; I said, “I will be wise,” but it was far from me.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 7:24} % *ECCLESIASTES-7-24
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-7-24}

			That which is far off, and exceeding deep, who can find it out?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רָח֖וֹק מַה־שֶּׁהָיָ֑ה וְעָמֹ֥ק ׀ עָמֹ֖ק מִ֥י יִמְצָאֶֽנּוּ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Πάντα ταῦτα ἐπείρασα ἐν σοφίᾳ. εἶπα Σοφισθήσομαι·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] That which is, is far off and exceeding deep; who can find it out?
					\item[NIV] Whatever exists is far off and most profound— who can discover it?
					\item[NRSV] That which is, is far off, and deep, very deep; who can find it out?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 7:25} % *ECCLESIASTES-7-25
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-7-25}

			I applied mine heart to know, and to search, and to seek out wisdom, and the reason of things, and to know the wickedness of folly, even of foolishness and madness:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{סַבּ֨וֹתִֽי אֲנִ֤י וְלִבִּי֙ לָדַ֣עַת וְלָת֔וּר וּבַקֵּ֥שׁ חָכְמָ֖ה וְחֶשְׁבּ֑וֹן וְלָדַ֙עַת֙ רֶ֣שַׁע כֶּ֔סֶל וְהַסִּכְל֖וּת הוֹלֵלֽוֹת׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ αὕτη ἐμακρύνθη ἀπ᾽ ἐμοῦ· μακρὰν ὑπὲρ ὃ ἦν, καὶ βαθὺ βάθος, τίς εὑρήσει αὐτό;}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] I turned about, and my heart was set to know and to search out, and to seek wisdom and the reason of things, and to know that wickedness is folly, and that foolishness is madness.
					\item[NIV] So I turned my mind to understand, to investigate and to search out wisdom and the scheme of things and to understand the stupidity of wickedness and the madness of folly.
					\item[NRSV] I turned my mind to know and to search out and to seek wisdom and the sum of things, and to know that wickedness is folly and that foolishness is madness.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 7:26} % *ECCLESIASTES-7-26
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-7-26}

			And I find more bitter than death the woman, whose heart is snares and nets, and her hands as bands: whoso pleaseth God shall escape from her; but the sinner shall be taken by her.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וּמוֹצֶ֨א אֲנִ֜י מַ֣ר מִמָּ֗וֶת אֶת־הָֽאִשָּׁה֙ אֲשֶׁר־הִ֨יא מְצוֹדִ֧ים וַחֲרָמִ֛ים לִבָּ֖הּ אֲסוּרִ֣ים יָדֶ֑יהָ ט֞וֹב לִפְנֵ֤י הָאֱלֹהִים֙ יִמָּלֵ֣ט מִמֶּ֔נָּה וְחוֹטֵ֖א יִלָּ֥כֶד בָּֽהּ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἐκύκλωσα ἐγώ, καὶ ἡ καρδία μου τοῦ γνῶναι καὶ τοῦ κατασκέψασθαι καὶ ζητῆσαι σοφίαν καὶ ψῆφον, καὶ τοῦ γνῶναι ἀσεβοῦς ἀφροσύνην καὶ ὀχληρίαν καὶ περιφοράν·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And I find more bitter than death the woman whose heart is snares and nets, and whose hands are bands: whoso pleaseth God shall escape from her; but the sinner shall be taken by her.
					\item[NIV] I find more bitter than death the woman who is a snare, whose heart is a trap and whose hands are chains. The man who pleases God will escape her, but the sinner she will ensnare.
					\item[NRSV] I found more bitter than death the woman who is a trap, whose heart is snares and nets, whose hands are fetters; one who pleases God escapes her, but the sinner is taken by her.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 7:27} % *ECCLESIASTES-7-27
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-7-27}

			Behold, this have I found, saith the preacher, counting one by one, to find out the account:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רְאֵה֙ זֶ֣ה מָצָ֔אתִי אָמְרָ֖ה קֹהֶ֑לֶת אַחַ֥ת לְאַחַ֖ת לִמְצֹ֥א חֶשְׁבּֽוֹן׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εὑρίσκω ἐγὼ αὐτήν, καὶ ἐρῶ πικρότερον ὑπὲρ θάνατον· σὺν τὴν γυναῖκα ἥτις ἐστὶν θήρευμα, καὶ σαγῆναι καρδία αὐτῆς, δεσμὸς εἰς χεῖρας αὐτῆς· ἀγαθὸς πρὸ προσώπου τοῦ θεοῦ ἐξαιρεθήσεται ἀπ᾽ αὐτῆς, καὶ ἁμαρτάνων συλλημφθήσεται ἐν αὐτῇ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Behold, this have I found, saith the Preacher, laying one thing to another, to find out the account;
					\item[NIV] “Look,” says the Teacher, “this is what I have discovered: “Adding one thing to another to discover the scheme of things—
					\item[NRSV] See, this is what I found, says the Teacher, adding one thing to another to find the sum,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 7:28} % *ECCLESIASTES-7-28
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-7-28}

			Which yet my soul seeketh, but I find not: one man among a thousand have I found; but a woman among all those have I not found.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֲשֶׁ֛ר עוֹד־בִּקְשָׁ֥ה נַפְשִׁ֖י וְלֹ֣א מָצָ֑אתִי אָדָ֞ם אֶחָ֤ד מֵאֶ֙לֶף֙ מָצָ֔אתִי וְאִשָּׁ֥ה בְכָל־אֵ֖לֶּה לֹ֥א מָצָֽאתִי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἴδε τοῦτο εὗρον, εἶπεν ὁ ἐκκλησιαστής, μία τῇ μιᾷ, τοῦ εὑρεῖν λογισμὸν}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] which my soul still seeketh, but I have not found: one man among a thousand have I found; but a woman among all those have I not found.
					\item[NIV] while I was still searching but not finding— I found one upright man among a thousand, but not one upright woman among them all.
					\item[NRSV] which my mind has sought repeatedly, but I have not found. One man among a thousand I found, but a woman among all these I have not found.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 7:29} % *ECCLESIASTES-7-29
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-7-29}

			Lo, this only have I found, that God hath made man upright; but they have sought out many inventions.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לְבַד֙ רְאֵה־זֶ֣ה מָצָ֔אתִי אֲשֶׁ֨ר עָשָׂ֧ה הָאֱלֹהִ֛ים אֶת־הָאָדָ֖ם יָשָׁ֑ר וְהֵ֥מָּה בִקְשׁ֖וּ חִשְּׁבֹנ֥וֹת רַבִּֽים׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὃν ἐπεζήτησεν ἡ ψυχή μου, καὶ οὐχ εὗρον· καὶ ἄνθρωπον ἕνα ἀπὸ χιλίων εὗρον, καὶ γυναῖκα ἐν πᾶσι τούτοις οὐχ εὗρον.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Behold, this only have I found: that God made man upright; but they have sought out many inventions.
					\item[NIV] This only have I found: God created mankind upright, but they have gone in search of many schemes.”
					\item[NRSV] See, this alone I found, that God made human beings straightforward, but they have devised many schemes.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Ecclesiastes 8} % *ECCLESIASTES-8
	% -----
		\label{ECCLESIASTES-8}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 8:1} % *ECCLESIASTES-8-1
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-8-1}

			Who is as the wise man? and who knoweth the interpretation of a thing? a man’s wisdom maketh his face to shine, and the boldness of his face shall be changed.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִ֚י כְּהֶ֣חָכָ֔ם וּמִ֥י יוֹדֵ֖עַ פֵּ֣שֶׁר דָּבָ֑ר חָכְמַ֤ת אָדָם֙ תָּאִ֣יר פָּנָ֔יו וְעֹ֥ז פָּנָ֖יו יְשֻׁנֶּֽא׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{σοφία ἀνθρώπου φωτιεῖ πρόσωπον αὐτοῦ, καὶ ἀναιδὴς προσώπῳ αὐτοῦ μισηθήσεται.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Who is as the wise man? and who knoweth the interpretation of a thing? A man's wisdom maketh his face to shine, and the hardness of his face is changed.
					\item[NIV] Who is like the wise? Who knows the explanation of things? A person’s wisdom brightens their face and changes its hard appearance.
					\item[NRSV] Who is like the wise man? And who knows the interpretation of a thing? Wisdom makes one’s face shine, and the hardness of one’s countenance is changed.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 8:2} % *ECCLESIASTES-8-2
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-8-2}

			I counsel thee to keep the king’s commandment, and that in regard of the oath of God.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֲנִי֙ פִּי־מֶ֣לֶךְ שְׁמ֔וֹר וְעַ֕ל דִּבְרַ֖ת שְׁבוּעַ֥ת אֱלֹהִֽים׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{στόμα βασιλέως φύλαξον, καὶ περὶ λόγου ὅρκου θεοῦ μὴ σπουδάσῃς·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] I counsel thee, Keep the king's command, and that in regard of the oath of God.
					\item[NIV] Obey the king’s command, I say, because you took an oath before God.
					\item[NRSV] Keep the king’s command because of your sacred oath.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 8:3} % *ECCLESIASTES-8-3
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-8-3}

			Be not hasty to go out of his sight: stand not in an evil thing; for he doeth whatsoever pleaseth him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תִּבָּהֵ֤ל מִפָּנָיו֙ תֵּלֵ֔ךְ אַֽל־תַּעֲמֹ֖ד בְּדָבָ֣ר רָ֑ע כִּ֛י כָּל־אֲשֶׁ֥ר יַחְפֹּ֖ץ יַעֲשֶֽׂה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἀπὸ προσώπου αὐτοῦ πορεύσῃ, μὴ στῇς ἐν λόγῳ πονηρῷ· ὅτι πᾶν ὃ ἐὰν θελήσει ποιήσει,}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Be not hasty to go out of his presence; persist not in an evil thing: for he doeth whatsoever pleaseth him.
					\item[NIV] Do not be in a hurry to leave the king’s presence. Do not stand up for a bad cause, for he will do whatever he pleases.
					\item[NRSV] Do not be terrified; go from his presence, do not delay when the matter is unpleasant, for he does whatever he pleases.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 8:4} % *ECCLESIASTES-8-4
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-8-4}

			Where the word of a king is, there is power: and who may say unto him, What doest thou?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר דְּבַר־מֶ֖לֶךְ שִׁלְט֑וֹן וּמִ֥י יֹֽאמַר־ל֖וֹ מַֽה־תַּעֲשֶֽׂה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καθὼς βασιλεὺς ἐξουσιάζων, καὶ τίς ἐρεῖ αὐτῷ Τί ποιεῖς;}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For the king's word hath power; and who may say unto him, What doest thou?
					\item[NIV] Since a king’s word is supreme, who can say to him, “What are you doing?”
					\item[NRSV] For the word of the king is powerful, and who can say to him, “What are you doing?”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 8:5} % *ECCLESIASTES-8-5
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-8-5}

			Whoso keepeth the commandment shall feel no evil thing: and a wise man’s heart discerneth both time and judgment.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שׁוֹמֵ֣ר מִצְוָ֔ה לֹ֥א יֵדַ֖ע דָּבָ֣ר רָ֑ע וְעֵ֣ת וּמִשְׁפָּ֔ט יֵדַ֖ע לֵ֥ב חָכָֽם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὁ φυλάσσων ἐντολὴν οὐ γνώσεται ῥῆμα πονηρόν, καὶ καιρὸν κρίσεως γινώσκει καρδία σοφοῦ·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Whoso keepeth the commandment shall know no evil thing; and a wise man's heart discerneth time and judgment:
					\item[NIV] Whoever obeys his command will come to no harm, and the wise heart will know the proper time and procedure.
					\item[NRSV] Whoever obeys a command will meet no harm, and the wise mind will know the time and way.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 8:6} % *ECCLESIASTES-8-6
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-8-6}

			Because to every purpose there is time and judgment, therefore the misery of man is great upon him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֣י לְכָל־חֵ֔פֶץ יֵ֖שׁ עֵ֣ת וּמִשְׁפָּ֑ט כִּֽי־רָעַ֥ת הָאָדָ֖ם רַבָּ֥ה עָלָֽיו׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὅτι παντὶ πράγματί ἐστιν καιρὸς καὶ κρίσις, ὅτι γνῶσις τοῦ ἀνθρώπου πολλὴ ἐπ᾽ αὐτόν·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] for to every purpose there is a time and judgment; because the misery of man is great upon him:
					\item[NIV] For there is a proper time and procedure for every matter, though a person may be weighed down by misery.
					\item[NRSV] For every matter has its time and way, although the troubles of mortals lie heavy upon them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 8:7} % *ECCLESIASTES-8-7
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-8-7}

			For he knoweth not that which shall be: for who can tell him when it shall be?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּֽי־אֵינֶ֥נּוּ יֹדֵ֖עַ מַה־שֶּׁיִּֽהְיֶ֑ה כִּ֚י כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר יִֽהְיֶ֔ה מִ֖י יַגִּ֥יד לֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὅτι οὐκ ἔστιν γινώσκων τί τὸ ἐσόμενον, ὅτι καθὼς ἔσται τίς ἀναγγελεῖ αὐτῷ;}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] for he knoweth not that which shall be; for who can tell him how it shall be?
					\item[NIV] Since no one knows the future, who can tell someone else what is to come?
					\item[NRSV] Indeed, they do not know what is to be, for who can tell them how it will be?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 8:8} % *ECCLESIASTES-8-8
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-8-8}

			There is no man that hath power over the spirit to retain the spirit; neither hath he power in the day of death: and there is no discharge in that war; neither shall wickedness deliver those that are given to it.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֵ֣ין אָדָ֞ם שַׁלִּ֤יט בָּר֙וּחַ֙ לִכְל֣וֹא אֶת־הָר֔וּחַ וְאֵ֤ין שִׁלְטוֹן֙ בְּי֣וֹם הַמָּ֔וֶת וְאֵ֥ין מִשְׁלַ֖חַת בַּמִּלְחָמָ֑ה וְלֹֽא־יְמַלֵּ֥ט רֶ֖שַׁע אֶת־בְּעָלָֽיו׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{οὐκ ἔστιν ἄνθρωπος ἐξουσιάζων ἐν πνεύματι τοῦ κωλῦσαι σὺν τὸ πνεῦμα· καὶ οὐκ ἔστιν ἐξουσία ἐν ἡμέρᾳ θανάτου, καὶ οὐκ ἔστιν ἀποστολὴ ἐν ἡμέρᾳ πολέμου, καὶ οὐ διασώσει ἀσέβεια τὸν παρ᾽ αὐτῆς.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] There is no man that hath power over the spirit to retain the spirit; neither hath he power over the day of death; and there is no discharge in war: neither shall wickedness deliver him that is given to it.
					\item[NIV] As no one has power over the wind to contain it, so no one has power over the time of their death. As no one is discharged in time of war, so wickedness will not release those who practice it.
					\item[NRSV] No one has power over the wind to restrain the wind, or power over the day of death; there is no discharge from the battle, nor does wickedness deliver those who practice it.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 8:9} % *ECCLESIASTES-8-9
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-8-9}

			All this have I seen, and applied my heart unto every work that is done under the sun: there is a time wherein one man ruleth over another to his own hurt.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֶת־כָּל־זֶ֤ה רָאִ֙יתִי֙ וְנָת֣וֹן אֶת־לִבִּ֔י לְכָֽל־מַעֲשֶׂ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר נַעֲשָׂ֖ה תַּ֣חַת הַשָּׁ֑מֶשׁ עֵ֗ת אֲשֶׁ֨ר שָׁלַ֧ט הָאָדָ֛ם בְּאָדָ֖ם לְרַ֥ע לֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ σύμπαν τοῦτο εἶδον, καὶ ἔδωκα τὴν καρδίαν μου εἰς πᾶν τὸ ποίημα ὃ πεποίηται ὑπὸ τὸν ἥλιον, τὰ ὅσα ἐξουσιάσατο ὁ ἄνθρωπος ἐν ἀνθρώπῳ τοῦ κακῶσαι αὐτόν·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] All this have I seen, and applied my heart unto every work that is done under the sun: there is a time wherein one man hath power over another to his hurt.
					\item[NIV] All this I saw, as I applied my mind to everything done under the sun. There is a time when a man lords it over others to his own hurt.
					\item[NRSV] All this I observed, applying my mind to all that is done under the sun, while one person exercises authority over another to the other’s hurt.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 8:10} % *ECCLESIASTES-8-10
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-8-10}

			And so I saw the wicked buried, who had come and gone from the place of the holy, and they were forgotten in the city where they had so done: this is also vanity.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וּבְכֵ֡ן רָאִיתִי֩ רְשָׁעִ֨ים קְבֻרִ֜ים וָבָ֗אוּ וּמִמְּק֤וֹם קָדוֹשׁ֙ יְהַלֵּ֔כוּ וְיִֽשְׁתַּכְּח֥וּ בָעִ֖יר אֲשֶׁ֣ר כֵּן־עָשׂ֑וּ גַּם־זֶ֖ה הָֽבֶל׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ τότε ἴδον ἀσεβεῖς εἰς τάφους εἰσαχθέντας, καὶ ἐκ τοῦ ἁγίου· καὶ ἐπορεύθησαν καὶ ἐπῃνέθησαν ἐν τῇ πόλει, ὅτι οὕτως ἐποίησαν. καί γε τοῦτο ματαιότης.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] So I saw the wicked buried, and they came to the grave; and they that had done right went away from the holy place, and were forgotten in the city: this also is vanity.
					\item[NIV] Then too, I saw the wicked buried—those who used to come and go from the holy place and receive praise in the city where they did this. This too is meaningless.
					\item[NRSV] Then I saw the wicked buried; they used to go in and out of the holy place, and were praised in the city where they had done such things. This also is vanity.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 8:11} % *ECCLESIASTES-8-11
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-8-11}

			Because sentence against an evil work is not executed speedily, therefore the heart of the sons of men is fully set in them to do evil.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֲשֶׁר֙ אֵין־נַעֲשָׂ֣ה פִתְגָ֔ם מַעֲשֵׂ֥ה הָרָעָ֖ה מְהֵרָ֑ה עַל־כֵּ֡ן מָלֵ֞א לֵ֧ב בְּֽנֵי־הָאָדָ֛ם בָּהֶ֖ם לַעֲשׂ֥וֹת רָֽע׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὅτι οὐκ ἔστιν γινομένη ἀντίρρησις ἀπὸ τῶν ποιούντων τὸ πονηρὸν ταχύ, διὰ τοῦτο ἐπληροφορήθη καρδία υἱῶν τοῦ ἀνθρώπου ἐν αὐτοῖς τοῦ ποιῆσαι τὸ πονηρόν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Because sentence against an evil work is not executed speedily, therefore the heart of the sons of men is fully set in them to do evil.
					\item[NIV] When the sentence for a crime is not quickly carried out, people’s hearts are filled with schemes to do wrong.
					\item[NRSV] Because sentence against an evil deed is not executed speedily, the human heart is fully set to do evil.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 8:12} % *ECCLESIASTES-8-12
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-8-12}

			Though a sinner do evil an hundred times, and his days be prolonged, yet surely I know that it shall be well with them that fear God, which fear before him:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֲשֶׁ֣ר חֹטֶ֗א עֹשֶׂ֥ה רָ֛ע מְאַ֖ת וּמַאֲרִ֣יךְ ל֑וֹ כִּ֚י גַּם־יוֹדֵ֣עַ אָ֔נִי אֲשֶׁ֤ר יִהְיֶה־טּוֹב֙ לְיִרְאֵ֣י הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר יִֽירְא֖וּ מִלְּפָנָֽיו׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὃς ἥμαρτεν, ἐποίησεν τὸ πονηρὸν ἀπὸ τότε καὶ ἀπὸ μακρότητος αὐτῶν· ὅτι καί γε γινώσκω ἐγὼ ὅτι ἐστὶν ἀγαθὸν τοῖς φοβουμένοις τὸν θεόν, ὅπως φοβῶνται ἀπὸ προσώπου αὐτοῦ·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Though a sinner do evil a hundred times, and prolong his days, yet surely I know that it shall be well with them that fear God, that fear before him:
					\item[NIV] Although a wicked person who commits a hundred crimes may live a long time, I know that it will go better with those who fear God, who are reverent before him.
					\item[NRSV] Though sinners do evil a hundred times and prolong their lives, yet I know that it will be well with those who fear God, because they stand in fear before him,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 8:13} % *ECCLESIASTES-8-13
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-8-13}

			But it shall not be well with the wicked, neither shall he prolong his days, which are as a shadow; because he feareth not before God.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְטוֹב֙ לֹֽא־יִהְיֶ֣ה לָֽרָשָׁ֔ע וְלֹֽא־יַאֲרִ֥יךְ יָמִ֖ים כַּצֵּ֑ל אֲשֶׁ֛ר אֵינֶ֥נּוּ יָרֵ֖א מִלִּפְנֵ֥י אֱלֹהִֽים׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἀγαθὸν οὐκ ἔσται τῷ ἀσεβεῖ, καὶ οὐ μακρυνεῖ ἡμέρας ἐν σκιᾷ ὃς οὐκ ἔστιν φοβούμενος ἀπὸ προσώπου τοῦ θεοῦ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] but it shall not be well with the wicked, neither shall he prolong his days, which are as a shadow; because he feareth not before God.
					\item[NIV] Yet because the wicked do not fear God, it will not go well with them, and their days will not lengthen like a shadow.
					\item[NRSV] but it will not be well with the wicked, neither will they prolong their days like a shadow, because they do not stand in fear before God.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 8:14} % *ECCLESIASTES-8-14
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-8-14}

			There is a vanity which is done upon the earth; that there be just men, unto whom it happeneth according to the work of the wicked; again, there be wicked men, to whom it happeneth according to the work of the righteous: I said that this also is vanity.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יֶשׁ־הֶבֶל֮ אֲשֶׁ֣ר נַעֲשָׂ֣ה עַל־הָאָרֶץ֒ אֲשֶׁ֣ר ׀ יֵ֣שׁ צַדִּיקִ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֨ר מַגִּ֤יעַ אֲלֵהֶם֙ כְּמַעֲשֵׂ֣ה הָרְשָׁעִ֔ים וְיֵ֣שׁ רְשָׁעִ֔ים שֶׁמַּגִּ֥יעַ אֲלֵהֶ֖ם כְּמַעֲשֵׂ֣ה הַצַּדִּיקִ֑ים אָמַ֕רְתִּי שֶׁגַּם־זֶ֖ה הָֽבֶל׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἔστιν ματαιότης ἣ πεποίηται ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς, ὅτι εἰσὶ δίκαιοι ὅτι φθάνει ἐπ᾽ αὐτοὺς ὡς ποίημα τῶν ἀσεβῶν· καί εἰσιν ἀσεβεῖς ὅτι φθάνει πρὸς αὐτοὺς ὡς ποίημα τῶν δικαίων· εἶπα ὅτι καί γε τοῦτο ματαιότης.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] There is a vanity which is done upon the earth, that there are righteous men unto whom it happeneth according to the work of the wicked; again, there are wicked men to whom it happeneth according to the work of the righteous: I said that this also is vanity.
					\item[NIV] There is something else meaningless that occurs on earth: the righteous who get what the wicked deserve, and the wicked who get what the righteous deserve. This too, I say, is meaningless.
					\item[NRSV] There is a vanity that takes place on earth, that there are righteous people who are treated according to the conduct of the wicked, and there are wicked people who are treated according to the conduct of the righteous. I said that this also is vanity.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 8:15} % *ECCLESIASTES-8-15
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-8-15}

			Then I commended mirth, because a man hath no better thing under the sun, than to eat, and to drink, and to be merry: for that shall abide with him of his labour the days of his life, which God giveth him under the sun.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְשִׁבַּ֤חְתִּֽי אֲנִי֙ אֶת־הַשִּׂמְחָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר אֵֽין־ט֤וֹב לָֽאָדָם֙ תַּ֣חַת הַשֶּׁ֔מֶשׁ כִּ֛י אִם־לֶאֱכ֥וֹל וְלִשְׁתּ֖וֹת וְלִשְׂמ֑וֹחַ וְה֞וּא יִלְוֶ֣נּוּ בַעֲמָל֗וֹ יְמֵ֥י חַיָּ֛יו אֲשֶׁר־נָֽתַן־ל֥וֹ הָאֱלֹהִ֖ים תַּ֥חַת הַשָּֽׁמֶשׁ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἐπῄνεσα ἐγὼ σὺν τὴν εὐφροσύνην, ὡς δ᾽ οὐκ ἔστιν ἀγαθὸν τῷ ἀνθρώπῳ ὑπὸ τὸν ἥλιον, ὅτι εἰ μὴ τοῦ φαγεῖν καὶ τοῦ πιεῖν καὶ τοῦ εὐφρανθῆναι· καὶ αὐτὸ συμπροσέσται αὐτῷ ἐν μόχθῳ αὐτοῦ ἡμέρας ζωῆς αὐτοῦ ὅσας ἔδωκεν αὐτῷ ὁ θεὸς ὑπὸ τὸν ἥλιον.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then I commended mirth, because a man hath no better thing under the sun, than to eat, and to drink, and to be joyful: for that shall abide with him in his labor all the days of his life which God hath given him under the sun.
					\item[NIV] So I commend the enjoyment of life, because there is nothing better for a person under the sun than to eat and drink and be glad. Then joy will accompany them in their toil all the days of the life God has given them under the sun.
					\item[NRSV] So I commend enjoyment, for there is nothing better for people under the sun than to eat, and drink, and enjoy themselves, for this will go with them in their toil through the days of life that God gives them under the sun.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 8:16} % *ECCLESIASTES-8-16
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-8-16}

			When I applied mine heart to know wisdom, and to see the business that is done upon the earth: (for also there is that neither day nor night seeth sleep with his eyes:)

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כַּאֲשֶׁ֨ר נָתַ֤תִּי אֶת־לִבִּי֙ לָדַ֣עַת חָכְמָ֔ה וְלִרְאוֹת֙ אֶת־הָ֣עִנְיָ֔ן אֲשֶׁ֥ר נַעֲשָׂ֖ה עַל־הָאָ֑רֶץ כִּ֣י גַ֤ם בַּיּוֹם֙ וּבַלַּ֔יְלָה שֵׁנָ֕ה בְּעֵינָ֖יו אֵינֶ֥נּוּ רֹאֶֽה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Ἐν οἷς ἔδωκα τὴν καρδίαν μου γνῶναι τὴν σοφίαν καὶ τοῦ ἰδεῖν τὸν περισπασμὸν τὸν πεποιημένον ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς· ὅτι καὶ ἐν ἡμέρᾳ καὶ ἐν νυκτὶ ὕπνον ὀφθαλμοῖς αὐτοῦ οὐκ ἔστιν βλέπων·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] When I applied my heart to know wisdom, and to see the business that is done upon the earth ( for also there is that neither day nor night seeth sleep with his eyes),
					\item[NIV] When I applied my mind to know wisdom and to observe the labor that is done on earth—people getting no sleep day or night—
					\item[NRSV] When I applied my mind to know wisdom, and to see the business that is done on earth, how one’s eyes see sleep neither day nor night,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 8:17} % *ECCLESIASTES-8-17
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-8-17}

			Then I beheld all the work of God, that a man cannot find out the work that is done under the sun: because though a man labour to seek it out, yet he shall not find it; yea farther; though a wise man think to know it, yet shall he not be able to find it.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְרָאִיתִי֮ אֶת־כָּל־מַעֲשֵׂ֣ה הָאֱלֹהִים֒ כִּי֩ לֹ֨א יוּכַ֜ל הָאָדָ֗ם לִמְצוֹא֙ אֶת־הַֽמַּעֲשֶׂה֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר נַעֲשָׂ֣ה תַֽחַת־הַשֶּׁ֔מֶשׁ בְּ֠שֶׁל אֲשֶׁ֨ר יַעֲמֹ֧ל הָאָדָ֛ם לְבַקֵּ֖שׁ וְלֹ֣א יִמְצָ֑א וְגַ֨ם אִם־יֹאמַ֤ר הֶֽחָכָם֙ לָדַ֔עַת לֹ֥א יוּכַ֖ל לִמְצֹֽא׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἴδον σύμπαντα τὰ ποιήματα τοῦ θεοῦ, ὅτι οὐ δυνήσεται ἄνθρωπος τοῦ εὑρεῖν σὺν τὸ ποίημα τὸ πεποιημένον ὑπὸ τὸν ἥλιον· ὅσα ἂν μοχθήσῃ ἄνθρωπος τοῦ ζητῆσαι, καὶ οὐχ εὑρήσει· καί γε ὅσα ἂν εἴπῃ σοφὸς τοῦ γνῶναι, οὐ δυνήσεται τοῦ εὑρεῖν· ὅτι σύμπαν τοῦτο ἔδωκα εἰς καρδίαν μου, καὶ καρδίᾳ μου σύμπαν ἴδον τοῦτο·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] then I beheld all the work of God, that man cannot find out the work that is done under the sun: because however much a man labor to seek it out, yet he shall not find it; yea moreover, though a wise man think to know it, yet shall he not be able to find it.
					\item[NIV] then I saw all that God has done. No one can comprehend what goes on under the sun. Despite all their efforts to search it out, no one can discover its meaning. Even if the wise claim they know, they cannot really comprehend it.
					\item[NRSV] then I saw all the work of God, that no one can find out what is happening under the sun. However much they may toil in seeking, they will not find it out; even though those who are wise claim to know, they cannot find it out.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Ecclesiastes 9} % *ECCLESIASTES-9
	% -----
		\label{ECCLESIASTES-9}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 9:1} % *ECCLESIASTES-9-1
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-9-1}

			For all this I considered in my heart even to declare all this, that the righteous, and the wise, and their works, are in the hand of God: no man knoweth either love or hatred by all that is before them.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֣י אֶת־כָּל־זֶ֞ה נָתַ֤תִּי אֶל־לִבִּי֙ וְלָב֣וּר אֶת־כָּל־זֶ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר הַצַּדִּיקִ֧ים וְהַחֲכָמִ֛ים וַעֲבָדֵיהֶ֖ם בְּיַ֣ד הָאֱלֹהִ֑ים גַּֽם־אַהֲבָ֣ה גַם־שִׂנְאָ֗ה אֵ֤ין יוֹדֵ֙עַ֙ הָֽאָדָ֔ם הַכֹּ֖ל לִפְנֵיהֶֽם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὡς οἱ δίκαιοι καὶ οἱ σοφοὶ καὶ ἐργασίαι αὐτῶν ἐν χειρὶ τοῦ θεοῦ, καί γε ἀγάπην καί γε μῖσος οὐκ ἔστιν εἰδὼς ὁ ἄνθρωπος· τὰ πάντα πρὸ προσώπου αὐτῶν, ματαιότης ἐν τοῖς πᾶσιν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For all this I laid to my heart, even to explore all this: that the righteous, and the wise, and their works, are in the hand of God; whether it be love or hatred, man knoweth it not; all is before them.
					\item[NIV] So I reflected on all this and concluded that the righteous and the wise and what they do are in God’s hands, but no one knows whether love or hate awaits them.
					\item[NRSV] All this I laid to heart, examining it all, how the righteous and the wise and their deeds are in the hand of God; whether it is love or hate one does not know. Everything that confronts them
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 9:2} % *ECCLESIASTES-9-2
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-9-2}

			All things come alike to all: there is one event to the righteous, and to the wicked; to the good and to the clean, and to the unclean; to him that sacrificeth, and to him that sacrificeth not: as is the good, so is the sinner; and he that sweareth, as he that feareth an oath.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הַכֹּ֞ל כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר לַכֹּ֗ל מִקְרֶ֨ה אֶחָ֜ד לַצַּדִּ֤יק וְלָרָשָׁע֙ לַטּוֹב֙ וְלַטָּה֣וֹר וְלַטָּמֵ֔א וְלַזֹּבֵ֔חַ וְלַאֲשֶׁ֖ר אֵינֶ֣נּוּ זֹבֵ֑חַ כַּטּוֹב֙ כַּֽחֹטֶ֔א הַנִּשְׁבָּ֕ע כַּאֲשֶׁ֖ר שְׁבוּעָ֥ה יָרֵֽא׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{συνάντημα ἓν τῷ δικαίῳ καὶ τῷ ἀσεβεῖ, τῷ ἀγαθῷ καὶ τῷ κακῷ, καὶ τῷ καθαρῷ καὶ τῷ ἀκαθάρτῳ, καὶ τῷ θυσιάζοντι καὶ τῷ μὴ θυσιάζοντι. ὡς ὁ ἀγαθός, ὣς ὁ ἁμαρτάνων, ὣς ὁ ὀμνύων καθὼς ὁ τὸν ὅρκον φοβούμενος.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] All things come alike to all: there is one event to the righteous and to the wicked; to the good and to the clean and to the unclean; to him that sacrificeth and to him that sacrificeth not; as is the good, so is the sinner; and he that sweareth, as he that feareth an oath.
					\item[NIV] All share a common destiny—the righteous and the wicked, the good and the bad, the clean and the unclean, those who offer sacrifices and those who do not. As it is with the good, so with the sinful; as it is with those who take oaths, so with those who are afraid to take them.
					\item[NRSV] is vanity, since the same fate comes to all, to the righteous and the wicked, to the good and the evil, to the clean and the unclean, to those who sacrifice and those who do not sacrifice. As are the good, so are the sinners; those who swear are like those who shun an oath.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 9:3} % *ECCLESIASTES-9-3
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-9-3}

			This is an evil among all things that are done under the sun, that there is one event unto all: yea, also the heart of the sons of men is full of evil, and madness is in their heart while they live, and after that they go to the dead.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{זֶ֣ה ׀ רָ֗ע בְּכֹ֤ל אֲשֶֽׁר־נַעֲשָׂה֙ תַּ֣חַת הַשֶּׁ֔מֶשׁ כִּֽי־מִקְרֶ֥ה אֶחָ֖ד לַכֹּ֑ל וְגַ֣ם לֵ֣ב בְּֽנֵי־הָ֠אָדָם מָלֵא־רָ֨ע וְהוֹלֵל֤וֹת בִּלְבָבָם֙ בְּחַיֵּיהֶ֔ם וְאַחֲרָ֖יו אֶל־הַמֵּתִֽים׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{τοῦτο πονηρὸν ἐν παντὶ πεποιημένῳ ὑπὸ τὸν ἥλιον ὅτι συνάντημα ἓν τοῖς πᾶσιν· καί γε καρδία υἱῶν τοῦ ἀνθρώπου ἐπληρώθη πονηροῦ, καὶ περιφέρεια ἐν καρδίᾳ αὐτῶν ἐν ζωῇ αὐτῶν, καὶ ὀπίσω αὐτῶν πρὸς τοὺς νεκρούς.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] This is an evil in all that is done under the sun, that there is one event unto all: yea also, the heart of the sons of men is full of evil, and madness is in their heart while they live, and after that they go to the dead.
					\item[NIV] This is the evil in everything that happens under the sun: The same destiny overtakes all. The hearts of people, moreover, are full of evil and there is madness in their hearts while they live, and afterward they join the dead.
					\item[NRSV] This is an evil in all that happens under the sun, that the same fate comes to everyone. Moreover, the hearts of all are full of evil; madness is in their hearts while they live, and after that they go to the dead.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 9:4} % *ECCLESIASTES-9-4
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-9-4}

			For to him that is joined to all the living there is hope: for a living dog is better than a dead lion.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּי־מִי֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר יְחֻבַּ֔ר אֶ֥ל כָּל־הַחַיִּ֖ים יֵ֣שׁ בִּטָּח֑וֹן כִּֽי־לְכֶ֤לֶב חַי֙ ה֣וּא ט֔וֹב מִן־הָאַרְיֵ֖ה הַמֵּֽת׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὅτι τίς ὃς κοινωνεῖ πρὸς πάντας τοὺς ζῶντας; ἔστιν ἐλπίς· ὅτι ὁ κύων ὁ ζῶν αὐτὸς ἀγαθὸς ὑπὲρ τὸν λέοντα τὸν νεκρόν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For to him that is joined with all the living there is hope; for a living dog is better than a dead lion.
					\item[NIV] Anyone who is among the living has hope —even a live dog is better off than a dead lion!
					\item[NRSV] But whoever is joined with all the living has hope, for a living dog is better than a dead lion.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 9:5} % *ECCLESIASTES-9-5
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-9-5}

			For the living know that they shall die: but the dead know not any thing, neither have they any more a reward; for the memory of them is forgotten.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֧י הַֽחַיִּ֛ים יוֹדְעִ֖ים שֶׁיָּמֻ֑תוּ וְהַמֵּתִ֞ים אֵינָ֧ם יוֹדְעִ֣ים מְא֗וּמָה וְאֵֽין־ע֤וֹד לָהֶם֙ שָׂכָ֔ר כִּ֥י נִשְׁכַּ֖ח זִכְרָֽם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὅτι οἱ ζῶντες γνώσονται ὅτι ἀποθανοῦνται, καὶ οἱ νεκροὶ οὐκ εἰσὶν γινώσκοντες οὐδέν· καὶ οὐκ ἔστιν αὐτοῖς ἔτι μισθός, ὅτι ἐπελήσθη ἡ μνήμη αὐτῶν·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For the living know that they shall die: but the dead know not anything, neither have they any more a reward; for the memory of them is forgotten.
					\item[NIV] For the living know that they will die, but the dead know nothing; they have no further reward, and even their name is forgotten.
					\item[NRSV] The living know that they will die, but the dead know nothing; they have no more reward, and even the memory of them is lost.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 9:6} % *ECCLESIASTES-9-6
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-9-6}

			Also their love, and their hatred, and their envy, is now perished; neither have they any more a portion for ever in any thing that is done under the sun.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גַּ֣ם אַהֲבָתָ֧ם גַּם־שִׂנְאָתָ֛ם גַּם־קִנְאָתָ֖ם כְּבָ֣ר אָבָ֑דָה וְחֵ֨לֶק אֵין־לָהֶ֥ם עוֹד֙ לְעוֹלָ֔ם בְּכֹ֥ל אֲשֶֽׁר־נַעֲשָׂ֖ה תַּ֥חַת הַשָּֽׁמֶשׁ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καί γε ἀγάπη αὐτῶν καί γε μῖσος αὐτῶν καί γε ζῆλος αὐτῶν ἤδη ἀπώλετο, καί γε μερὶς οὐκ ἔστιν αὐτοῖς ἔτι εἰς αἰῶνα ἐν παντὶ τῷ πεποιημένῳ ὑπὸ τὸν ἥλιον.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] As well their love, as their hatred and their envy, is perished long ago; neither have they any more a portion for ever in anything that is done under the sun.
					\item[NIV] Their love, their hate and their jealousy have long since vanished; never again will they have a part in anything that happens under the sun.
					\item[NRSV] Their love and their hate and their envy have already perished; never again will they have any share in all that happens under the sun.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 9:7} % *ECCLESIASTES-9-7
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-9-7}

			Go thy way, eat thy bread with joy, and drink thy wine with a merry heart; for God now accepteth thy works.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֵ֣ךְ אֱכֹ֤ל בְּשִׂמְחָה֙ לַחְמֶ֔ךָ וּֽשֲׁתֵ֥ה בְלֶב־ט֖וֹב יֵינֶ֑ךָ כִּ֣י כְבָ֔ר רָצָ֥ה הָאֱלֹהִ֖ים אֶֽת־מַעֲשֶֽׂיךָ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Δεῦρο φάγε ἐν εὐφροσύνῃ ἄρτον σου, καὶ πίε ἐν καρδίᾳ ἀγαθῇ οἶνόν σου, ὅτι ἤδη εὐδόκησεν ὁ θεὸς τὰ ποιήματά σου.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Go thy way, eat thy bread with joy, and drink thy wine with a merry heart; for God hath already accepted thy works.
					\item[NIV] Go, eat your food with gladness, and drink your wine with a joyful heart, for God has already approved what you do.
					\item[NRSV] Go, eat your bread with enjoyment, and drink your wine with a merry heart; for God has long ago approved what you do.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 9:8} % *ECCLESIASTES-9-8
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-9-8}

			Let thy garments be always white; and let thy head lack no ointment.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּכָל־עֵ֕ת יִהְי֥וּ בְגָדֶ֖יךָ לְבָנִ֑ים וְשֶׁ֖מֶן עַל־רֹאשְׁךָ֥ אַל־יֶחְסָֽר׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἐν παντὶ καιρῷ ἔστωσαν ἱμάτιά σου λευκά, καὶ ἔλαιον ἐπὶ κεφαλήν σου μὴ ὑστερησάτω·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Let thy garments be always white; and let not thy head lack oil.
					\item[NIV] Always be clothed in white, and always anoint your head with oil.
					\item[NRSV] Let your garments always be white; do not let oil be lacking on your head.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 9:9} % *ECCLESIASTES-9-9
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-9-9}

			Live joyfully with the wife whom thou lovest all the days of the life of thy vanity, which he hath given thee under the sun, all the days of thy vanity: for that is thy portion in this life, and in thy labour which thou takest under the sun.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רְאֵ֨ה חַיִּ֜ים עִם־אִשָּׁ֣ה אֲשֶׁר־אָהַ֗בְתָּ כָּל־יְמֵי֙ חַיֵּ֣י הֶבְלֶ֔ךָ אֲשֶׁ֤ר נָֽתַן־לְךָ֙ תַּ֣חַת הַשֶּׁ֔מֶשׁ כֹּ֖ל יְמֵ֣י הֶבְלֶ֑ךָ כִּ֣י ה֤וּא חֶלְקְךָ֙ בַּֽחַיִּ֔ים וּבַעֲמָ֣לְךָ֔ אֲשֶׁר־אַתָּ֥ה עָמֵ֖ל תַּ֥חַת הַשָּֽׁמֶשׁ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ἴδε ζωὴν μετὰ γυναικὸς ἧς ἠγάπησας πάσας ἡμέρας ζωῆς ματαιότητός σου τὰς δοθείσας σοι ὑπὸ τὸν ἥλιον. πᾶσαι ἡμέραι ἡμέραι ἀτμοῦ σου· ὅτι αὐτὸ μερίς σου ἐν τῇ ζωῇ σου καὶ ἐν τῷ μόχθῳ σου ᾧ σὺ μοχθεῖς ὑπὸ τὸν ἥλιον.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Live joyfully with the wife whom thou lovest all the days of thy life of vanity, which he hath given thee under the sun, all thy days of vanity: for that is thy portion in life, and in thy labor wherein thou laborest under the sun.
					\item[NIV] Enjoy life with your wife, whom you love, all the days of this meaningless life that God has given you under the sun—all your meaningless days. For this is your lot in life and in your toilsome labor under the sun.
					\item[NRSV] Enjoy life with the wife whom you love, all the days of your vain life that are given you under the sun, because that is your portion in life and in your toil at which you toil under the sun.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 9:10} % *ECCLESIASTES-9-10
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-9-10}

			Whatsoever thy hand findeth to do, do it with thy might; for there is no work, nor device, nor knowledge, nor wisdom, in the grave, whither thou goest.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כֹּ֠ל אֲשֶׁ֨ר תִּמְצָ֧א יָֽדְךָ֛ לַעֲשׂ֥וֹת בְּכֹחֲךָ֖ עֲשֵׂ֑ה כִּי֩ אֵ֨ין מַעֲשֶׂ֤ה וְחֶשְׁבּוֹן֙ וְדַ֣עַת וְחָכְמָ֔ה בִּשְׁא֕וֹל אֲשֶׁ֥ר אַתָּ֖ה הֹלֵ֥ךְ שָֽׁמָּה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{πάντα ὅσα ἂν εὕρῃ ἡ χείρ σου τοῦ ποιῆσαι, ὡς ἡ δύναμίς σου ποίησον, ὅτι οὐκ ἔστιν ποίημα καὶ λογισμὸς καὶ γνῶσις καὶ σοφία ἐν ᾅδῃ, ὅπου σὺ πορεύῃ ἐκεῖ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Whatsoever thy hand findeth to do, do it with thy might; for there is no work, nor device, nor knowledge, nor wisdom, in Sheol, whither thou goest.
					\item[NIV] Whatever your hand finds to do, do it with all your might, for in the realm of the dead, where you are going, there is neither working nor planning nor knowledge nor wisdom.
					\item[NRSV] Whatever your hand finds to do, do with your might; for there is no work or thought or knowledge or wisdom in Sheol, to which you are going.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 9:11} % *ECCLESIASTES-9-11
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-9-11}

			I returned, and saw under the sun, that the race is not to the swift, nor the battle to the strong, neither yet bread to the wise, nor yet riches to men of understanding, nor yet favour to men of skill; but time and chance happeneth to them all.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שַׁ֜בְתִּי וְרָאֹ֣ה תַֽחַת־הַשֶּׁ֗מֶשׁ כִּ֣י לֹא֩ לַקַּלִּ֨ים הַמֵּר֜וֹץ וְלֹ֧א לַגִּבּוֹרִ֣ים הַמִּלְחָמָ֗ה וְ֠גַם לֹ֣א לַחֲכָמִ֥ים לֶ֙חֶם֙ וְגַ֨ם לֹ֤א לַנְּבֹנִים֙ עֹ֔שֶׁר וְגַ֛ם לֹ֥א לַיֹּדְעִ֖ים חֵ֑ן כִּי־עֵ֥ת וָפֶ֖גַע יִקְרֶ֥ה אֶת־כֻּלָּֽם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Ἐπέστρεψα καὶ ἴδον ὑπὸ τὸν ἥλιον ὅτι οὐ τοῖς κούφοις δρόμος καὶ οὐ τοῖς δυνατοῖς πόλεμος, καί γε οὐ τῷ σοφῷ ἄρτος καὶ οὐ τοῖς συνετοῖς πλοῦτος, καί γε οὐ τοῖς γινώσκουσιν χάρις· ὅτι καιρὸς καὶ ἀπάντημα συνατήσεται σύμπασιν αὐτοῖς.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] I returned, and saw under the sun, that the race is not to the swift, nor the battle to the strong, neither yet bread to the wise, nor yet riches to men of understanding, nor yet favor to men of skill; but time and chance happeneth to them all.
					\item[NIV] I have seen something else under the sun: The race is not to the swift or the battle to the strong, nor does food come to the wise or wealth to the brilliant or favor to the learned; but time and chance happen to them all.
					\item[NRSV] Again I saw that under the sun the race is not to the swift, nor the battle to the strong, nor bread to the wise, nor riches to the intelligent, nor favor to the skillful; but time and chance happen to them all.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 9:12} % *ECCLESIASTES-9-12
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-9-12}

			For man also knoweth not his time: as the fishes that are taken in an evil net, and as the birds that are caught in the snare; so are the sons of men snared in an evil time, when it falleth suddenly upon them.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֡י גַּם֩ לֹֽא־יֵדַ֨ע הָאָדָ֜ם אֶת־עִתּ֗וֹ כַּדָּגִים֙ שֶׁנֶּֽאֱחָזִים֙ בִּמְצוֹדָ֣ה רָעָ֔ה וְכַ֨צִּפֳּרִ֔ים הָאֲחֻז֖וֹת בַּפָּ֑ח כָּהֵ֗ם יֽוּקָשִׁים֙ בְּנֵ֣י הָֽאָדָ֔ם לְעֵ֣ת רָעָ֔ה כְּשֶׁתִּפּ֥וֹל עֲלֵיהֶ֖ם פִּתְאֹֽם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὅτι καί γε καὶ οὐκ ἔγνω ὁ ἄνθρωπος τὸν καιρὸν αὐτοῦ· ὡς οἱ ἰχθύες οἱ θηρευόμενοι ἐν ἀμφιβλήστρῳ καλῷ, καὶ ὡς ὄρνεα τὰ θηρευόμενα ἐν παγίδι, ὡς αὐτὰ παγιδεύονται οἱ υἱοὶ τοῦ ἀνθρώπου εἰς καιρὸν πονηρόν, ὅταν ἐπιπέσῃ ἐπ᾽ αὐτοὺς ἄφνω.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For man also knoweth not his time: as the fishes that are taken in an evil net, and as the birds that are caught in the snare, even so are the sons of men snared in an evil time, when it falleth suddenly upon them.
					\item[NIV] Moreover, no one knows when their hour will come: As fish are caught in a cruel net, or birds are taken in a snare, so people are trapped by evil times that fall unexpectedly upon them.
					\item[NRSV] For no one can anticipate the time of disaster. Like fish taken in a cruel net, and like birds caught in a snare, so mortals are snared at a time of calamity, when it suddenly falls upon them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 9:13} % *ECCLESIASTES-9-13
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-9-13}

			This wisdom have I seen also under the sun, and it seemed great unto me:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גַּם־זֹ֛ה רָאִ֥יתִי חָכְמָ֖ה תַּ֣חַת הַשָּׁ֑מֶשׁ וּגְדוֹלָ֥ה הִ֖יא אֵלָֽי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Καί γε τοῦτο ἴδον σοφίαν ὑπὸ τὸν ἥλιον, καὶ μεγάλη ἐστὶν πρὸς μέ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] I have also seen wisdom under the sun on this wise, and it seemed great unto me:
					\item[NIV] I also saw under the sun this example of wisdom that greatly impressed me:
					\item[NRSV] I have also seen this example of wisdom under the sun, and it seemed great to me.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 9:14} % *ECCLESIASTES-9-14
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-9-14}

			There was a little city, and few men within it; and there came a great king against it, and besieged it, and built great bulwarks against it:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עִ֣יר קְטַנָּ֔ה וַאֲנָשִׁ֥ים בָּ֖הּ מְעָ֑ט וּבָֽא־אֵלֶ֜יהָ מֶ֤לֶךְ גָּדוֹל֙ וְסָבַ֣ב אֹתָ֔הּ וּבָנָ֥ה עָלֶ֖יהָ מְצוֹדִ֥ים גְּדֹלִֽים׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{πόλις μικρὰ καὶ ἄνδρες ἐν αὐτῇ ὀλίγοι, καὶ ἔλθῃ ἐπ᾽ αὐτὴν βασιλεὺς μέγας καὶ κυκλώσῃ αὐτὴν καὶ οἰκοδομήσῃ ἐπ᾽ αὐτὴν χάρακας μεγάλους,}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] There was a little city, and few men within it; and there came a great king against it, and besieged it, and built great bulwarks against it.
					\item[NIV] There was once a small city with only a few people in it. And a powerful king came against it, surrounded it and built huge siege works against it.
					\item[NRSV] There was a little city with few people in it. A great king came against it and besieged it, building great siegeworks against it.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 9:15} % *ECCLESIASTES-9-15
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-9-15}

			Now there was found in it a poor wise man, and he by his wisdom delivered the city; yet no man remembered that same poor man.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וּמָ֣צָא בָ֗הּ אִ֤ישׁ מִסְכֵּן֙ חָכָ֔ם וּמִלַּט־ה֥וּא אֶת־הָעִ֖יר בְּחָכְמָת֑וֹ וְאָדָם֙ לֹ֣א זָכַ֔ר אֶת־הָאִ֥ישׁ הַמִּסְכֵּ֖ן הַהּֽוּא׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εὕρῃ ἐν αὐτῇ ἄνδρα πένητα σοφόν, καὶ διασώσῃ αὐτὸς τὴν πόλιν ἐν τῇ σοφίᾳ αὐτοῦ, καὶ ἄνθρωπος οὐκ ἐμνήσθη σὺν τοῦ ἀνδρὸς τοῦ πένητος ἐκείνου.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Now there was found in it a poor wise man, and he by his wisdom delivered the city; yet no man remembered that same poor man.
					\item[NIV] Now there lived in that city a man poor but wise, and he saved the city by his wisdom. But nobody remembered that poor man.
					\item[NRSV] Now there was found in it a poor wise man, and he by his wisdom delivered the city. Yet no one remembered that poor man.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 9:16} % *ECCLESIASTES-9-16
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-9-16}

			Then said I, Wisdom is better than strength: nevertheless the poor man’s wisdom is despised, and his words are not heard.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְאָמַ֣רְתִּי אָ֔נִי טוֹבָ֥ה חָכְמָ֖ה מִגְּבוּרָ֑ה וְחָכְמַ֤ת הַמִּסְכֵּן֙ בְּזוּיָ֔ה וּדְבָרָ֖יו אֵינָ֥ם נִשְׁמָעִֽים׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ εἶπα ἐγώ Ἀγαθὴ σοφία ὑπὲρ δύναμιν· καὶ σοφία τοῦ πένητος ἐξουδενωμένη, καὶ οἱ λόγοι αὐτοῦ οὐκ εἰσακουόμενοι,}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then said I, Wisdom is better than strength: nevertheless the poor man's wisdom is despised, and his words are not heard.
					\item[NIV] So I said, “Wisdom is better than strength.” But the poor man’s wisdom is despised, and his words are no longer heeded.
					\item[NRSV] So I said, “Wisdom is better than might; yet the poor man’s wisdom is despised, and his words are not heeded.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 9:17} % *ECCLESIASTES-9-17
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-9-17}

			The words of wise men are heard in quiet more than the cry of him that ruleth among fools.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{דִּבְרֵ֣י חֲכָמִ֔ים בְּנַ֖חַת נִשְׁמָעִ֑ים מִזַּעֲקַ֥ת מוֹשֵׁ֖ל בַּכְּסִילִֽים׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{Λόγοι σοφῶν ἐν ἀναπαύσει ἀκούονται ὑπὲρ κραυγὴν ἐξουσιαζόντων ἐν ἀφροσύναις.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The words of the wise heard in quiet are better than the cry of him that ruleth among fools.
					\item[NIV] The quiet words of the wise are more to be heeded than the shouts of a ruler of fools.
					\item[NRSV] The quiet words of the wise are more to be heeded than the shouting of a ruler among fools.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 9:18} % *ECCLESIASTES-9-18
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-9-18}

			Wisdom is better than weapons of war: but one sinner destroyeth much good.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{טוֹבָ֥ה חָכְמָ֖ה מִכְּלֵ֣י קְרָ֑ב וְחוֹטֶ֣א אֶחָ֔ד יְאַבֵּ֥ד טוֹבָ֥ה הַרְבֵּֽה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἀγαθὴ σοφία ὑπὲρ σκεύη πολέμου, καὶ ἁμαρτάνων εἷς ἀπολέσει ἀγαθωσύνην πολλήν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Wisdom is better than weapons of war; but one sinner destroyeth much good.
					\item[NIV] Wisdom is better than weapons of war, but one sinner destroys much good.
					\item[NRSV] Wisdom is better than weapons of war, but one bungler destroys much good.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Ecclesiastes 10} % *ECCLESIASTES-10
	% -----
		\label{ECCLESIASTES-10}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 10:1} % *ECCLESIASTES-10-1
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-10-1}

			Dead flies cause the ointment of the apothecary to send forth a stinking savour: so doth a little folly him that is in reputation for wisdom and honour.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{זְב֣וּבֵי מָ֔וֶת יַבְאִ֥ישׁ יַבִּ֖יעַ שֶׁ֣מֶן רוֹקֵ֑חַ יָקָ֛ר מֵחָכְמָ֥ה מִכָּב֖וֹד סִכְל֥וּת מְעָֽט׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{μυῖαι θανατοῦσαι σαπριοῦσιν σκευασίαν, ἔλαιον ἡδύσματος· τίμιον ὁ λόγος σοφίας ὑπὲρ δόξαν ἀφροσύνης μεγάλης.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Dead flies cause the oil of the perfumer to send forth an evil odor; so doth a little folly outweigh wisdom and honor.
					\item[NIV] As dead flies give perfume a bad smell, so a little folly outweighs wisdom and honor.
					\item[NRSV] Dead flies make the perfumer’s ointment give off a foul odor; so a little folly outweighs wisdom and honor.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 10:2} % *ECCLESIASTES-10-2
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-10-2}

			A wise man’s heart is at his right hand; but a fool’s heart at his left.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֵ֤ב חָכָם֙ לִֽימִינ֔וֹ וְלֵ֥ב כְּסִ֖יל לִשְׂמֹאלֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καρδία σοφοῦ εἰς δεξιὸν αὐτοῦ. καὶ καρδία ἄφρονος εἰς ἀριστερὸν αὐτοῦ.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A wise man's heart is at his right hand; but a fool's heart at his left.
					\item[NIV] The heart of the wise inclines to the right, but the heart of the fool to the left.
					\item[NRSV] The heart of the wise inclines to the right, but the heart of a fool to the left.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 10:3} % *ECCLESIASTES-10-3
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-10-3}

			Yea also, when he that is a fool walketh by the way, his wisdom faileth him, and he saith to every one that he is a fool.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְגַם־בַּדֶּ֛רֶךְ כְּשֶׁסָּכָ֥ל הֹלֵ֖ךְ לִבּ֣וֹ חָסֵ֑ר וְאָמַ֥ר לַכֹּ֖ל סָכָ֥ל הֽוּא׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καί γε ἐν ὁδῷ ὅταν ἄφρων πορεύηται, καρδία αὐτοῦ ὑστερήσει καὶ ἃ λογιεῖται πάντα ἀφροσύνη ἐστίν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Yea also, when the fool walketh by the way, his understanding faileth him, and he saith to every one that he is a fool.
					\item[NIV] Even as fools walk along the road, they lack sense and show everyone how stupid they are.
					\item[NRSV] Even when fools walk on the road, they lack sense, and show to everyone that they are fools.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 10:4} % *ECCLESIASTES-10-4
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-10-4}

			If the spirit of the ruler rise up against thee, leave not thy place; for yielding pacifieth great offences.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִם־ר֤וּחַ הַמּוֹשֵׁל֙ תַּעֲלֶ֣ה עָלֶ֔יךָ מְקוֹמְךָ֖ אַל־תַּנַּ֑ח כִּ֣י מַרְפֵּ֔א יַנִּ֖יחַ חֲטָאִ֥ים גְּדוֹלִֽים׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἐὰν πνεῦμα τοῦ ἐξουσιάζοντος ἀναβῇ ἐπὶ σέ, τόπον σου μὴ ἀφῇς, ὅτι ἴαμα καταπαύσει ἁμαρτίας μεγάλας.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] If the spirit of the ruler rise up against thee, leave not thy place; for gentleness allayeth great offences.
					\item[NIV] If a ruler’s anger rises against you, do not leave your post; calmness can lay great offenses to rest.
					\item[NRSV] If the anger of the ruler rises against you, do not leave your post, for calmness will undo great offenses.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 10:5} % *ECCLESIASTES-10-5
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-10-5}

			There is an evil which I have seen under the sun, as an error which proceedeth from the ruler:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יֵ֣שׁ רָעָ֔ה רָאִ֖יתִי תַּ֣חַת הַשָּׁ֑מֶשׁ כִּשְׁגָגָ֕ה שֶׁיֹּצָ֖א מִלִּפְנֵ֥י הַשַּׁלִּֽיט׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἔστιν πονηρία ἣν ἴδον ὑπὸ τὸν ἥλιον, ὡς ἀκούσιον ἐξῆλθεν ἀπὸ προσώπου ἐξουσιάζοντος.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] There is an evil which I have seen under the sun, as it were an error which proceedeth from the ruler:
					\item[NIV] There is an evil I have seen under the sun, the sort of error that arises from a ruler:
					\item[NRSV] There is an evil that I have seen under the sun, as great an error as if it proceeded from the ruler:
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 10:6} % *ECCLESIASTES-10-6
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-10-6}

			Folly is set in great dignity, and the rich sit in low place.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נִתַּ֣ן הַסֶּ֔כֶל בַּמְּרוֹמִ֖ים רַבִּ֑ים וַעֲשִׁירִ֖ים בַּשֵּׁ֥פֶל יֵשֵֽׁבוּ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἐδόθη ὁ ἄφρων ἐν ὕψεσι μεγάλοις, καὶ πλούσιοι ἐν ταπεινῷ καθήσονται·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] folly is set in great dignity, and the rich sit in a low place.
					\item[NIV] Fools are put in many high positions, while the rich occupy the low ones.
					\item[NRSV] folly is set in many high places, and the rich sit in a low place.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 10:7} % *ECCLESIASTES-10-7
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-10-7}

			I have seen servants upon horses, and princes walking as servants upon the earth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רָאִ֥יתִי עֲבָדִ֖ים עַל־סוּסִ֑ים וְשָׂרִ֛ים הֹלְכִ֥ים כַּעֲבָדִ֖ים עַל־הָאָֽרֶץ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἴδον δούλους ἐφ᾽ ἵππους καὶ ἄρχοντας πορευομένους ὡς δούλους ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] I have seen servants upon horses, and princes walking like servants upon the earth.
					\item[NIV] I have seen slaves on horseback, while princes go on foot like slaves.
					\item[NRSV] I have seen slaves on horseback, and princes walking on foot like slaves.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 10:8} % *ECCLESIASTES-10-8
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-10-8}

			He that diggeth a pit shall fall into it; and whoso breaketh an hedge, a serpent shall bite him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חֹפֵ֥ר גּוּמָּ֖ץ בּ֣וֹ יִפּ֑וֹל וּפֹרֵ֥ץ גָּדֵ֖ר יִשְּׁכֶ֥נּוּ נָחָֽשׁ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ὁ ὀρύσσων βόθρον εἰς αὐτὸν ἐμπεσεῖται, καὶ καθαιροῦντα φραγμόν, δήξεται αὐτὸν ὄφις·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that diggeth a pit shall fall into it; and whoso breaketh through a wall, a serpent shall bite him.
					\item[NIV] Whoever digs a pit may fall into it; whoever breaks through a wall may be bitten by a snake.
					\item[NRSV] Whoever digs a pit will fall into it; and whoever breaks through a wall will be bitten by a snake.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 10:9} % *ECCLESIASTES-10-9
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-10-9}

			Whoso removeth stones shall be hurt therewith; and he that cleaveth wood shall be endangered thereby.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַסִּ֣יעַ אֲבָנִ֔ים יֵעָצֵ֖ב בָּהֶ֑ם בּוֹקֵ֥עַ עֵצִ֖ים יִסָּ֥כֶן בָּֽם׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἐξαίρων λίθους διαπονηθήσεται ἐν αὐτοῖς, σχίζων ξύλα κινδυνεύσει ἐν αὐτοῖς.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Whoso heweth out stones shall be hurt therewith; and he that cleaveth wood is endangered thereby.
					\item[NIV] Whoever quarries stones may be injured by them; whoever splits logs may be endangered by them.
					\item[NRSV] Whoever quarries stones will be hurt by them; and whoever splits logs will be endangered by them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 10:10} % *ECCLESIASTES-10-10
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-10-10}

			If the iron be blunt, and he do not whet the edge, then must he put to more strength: but wisdom is profitable to direct.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִם־קֵהָ֣ה הַבַּרְזֶ֗ל וְהוּא֙ לֹא־פָנִ֣ים קִלְקַ֔ל וַחֲיָלִ֖ים יְגַבֵּ֑ר וְיִתְר֥וֹן הַכְשֵׁ֖ר חָכְמָֽה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἐὰν ἐκπέσῃ τὸ σιδήριον, καὶ αὐτὸς πρόσωπον ἐτάραξεν· καὶ δυνάμεις δυναμώσει, καὶ περισσεία τῷ ἀνδρὶ οὐ σοφία·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] If the iron be blunt, and one do not whet the edge, then must he put to more strength: but wisdom is profitable to direct.
					\item[NIV] If the ax is dull and its edge unsharpened, more strength is needed, but skill will bring success.
					\item[NRSV] If the iron is blunt, and one does not whet the edge, then more strength must be exerted; but wisdom helps one to succeed.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 10:11} % *ECCLESIASTES-10-11
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-10-11}

			Surely the serpent will bite without enchantment; and a babbler is no better.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִם־יִשֹּׁ֥ךְ הַנָּחָ֖שׁ בְּלוֹא־לָ֑חַשׁ וְאֵ֣ין יִתְר֔וֹן לְבַ֖עַל הַלָּשֽׁוֹן׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἐὰν δάκῃ ὄφις ἐν οὐ ψιθυρισμῷ, καὶ οὐκ ἔστιν περισσεία τῷ ἐπᾴδοντι.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] If the serpent bite before it is charmed, then is there no advantage in the charmer.
					\item[NIV] If a snake bites before it is charmed, the charmer receives no fee.
					\item[NRSV] If the snake bites before it is charmed, there is no advantage in a charmer.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 10:12} % *ECCLESIASTES-10-12
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-10-12}

			The words of a wise man’s mouth are gracious; but the lips of a fool will swallow up himself.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{דִּבְרֵ֥י פִי־חָכָ֖ם חֵ֑ן וְשִׂפְת֥וֹת כְּסִ֖יל תְּבַלְּעֶֽנּוּ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{λόγοι στόματος σοφοῦ χάρις, καὶ χείλη ἄφρονος καταποντιοῦσιν αὐτόν·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The words of a wise man's mouth are gracious; but the lips of a fool will swallow up himself.
					\item[NIV] Words from the mouth of the wise are gracious, but fools are consumed by their own lips.
					\item[NRSV] Words spoken by the wise bring them favor, but the lips of fools consume them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 10:13} % *ECCLESIASTES-10-13
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-10-13}

			The beginning of the words of his mouth is foolishness: and the end of his talk is mischievous madness.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תְּחִלַּ֥ת דִּבְרֵי־פִ֖יהוּ סִכְל֑וּת וְאַחֲרִ֣ית פִּ֔יהוּ הוֹלֵל֖וּת רָעָֽה׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἀρχὴ λόγων στόματος αὐτοῦ ἀφροσύνη, καὶ ἐσχάτη αὐτοῦ περιφέρεια πονηρά·}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The beginning of the words of his mouth is foolishness; and the end of his talk is mischievous madness.
					\item[NIV] At the beginning their words are folly; at the end they are wicked madness—
					\item[NRSV] The words of their mouths begin in foolishness, and their talk ends in wicked madness;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 10:14} % *ECCLESIASTES-10-14
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-10-14}

			A fool also is full of words: a man cannot tell what shall be; and what shall be after him, who can tell him?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְהַסָּכָ֖ל יַרְבֶּ֣ה דְבָרִ֑ים לֹא־יֵדַ֤ע הָאָדָם֙ מַה־שֶׁיִּֽהְיֶ֔ה וַאֲשֶׁ֤ר יִֽהְיֶה֙ מֵֽאַחֲרָ֔יו מִ֖י יַגִּ֥יד לֽוֹ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καὶ ὁ ἄφρων πληθύνει λόγους. οὐκ ἔγνω ἄνθρωπος τί τὸ γενόμενον καὶ τί τὸ ἐσόμενον· τί ὀπίσω αὐτοῦ τίς ἀναγγελεῖ αὐτῷ;}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A fool also multiplieth words: yet man knoweth not what shall be; and that which shall be after him, who can tell him?
					\item[NIV] and fools multiply words. No one knows what is coming— who can tell someone else what will happen after them?
					\item[NRSV] yet fools talk on and on. No one knows what is to happen, and who can tell anyone what the future holds?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 10:15} % *ECCLESIASTES-10-15
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-10-15}

			The labour of the foolish wearieth every one of them, because he knoweth not how to go to the city.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֲמַ֥ל הַכְּסִילִ֖ים תְּיַגְּעֶ֑נּוּ אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹֽא־יָדַ֖ע לָלֶ֥כֶת אֶל־עִֽיר׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{μόχθος τῶν ἀφρόνων κακώσει αὐτούς, ὃς οὐκ ἔγνω τοῦ πορευθῆναι εἰς πόλιν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The labor of fools wearieth every one of them; for he knoweth not how to go to the city.
					\item[NIV] The toil of fools wearies them; they do not know the way to town.
					\item[NRSV] The toil of fools wears them out, for they do not even know the way to town.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 10:16} % *ECCLESIASTES-10-16
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-10-16}

			Woe to thee, O land, when thy king is a child, and thy princes eat in the morning!

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִֽי־לָ֣ךְ אֶ֔רֶץ שֶׁמַּלְכֵּ֖ךְ נָ֑עַר וְשָׂרַ֖יִךְ בַּבֹּ֥קֶר יֹאכֵֽלוּ׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{οὐαί σοι, πόλις, ἧς ὁ βασιλεύς σου νεώτερος, καὶ οἱ ἄρχοντές σου πρωὶ ἐσθίουσιν.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Woe to thee, O land, when thy king is a child, and thy princes eat in the morning!
					\item[NIV] Woe to the land whose king was a servant and whose princes feast in the morning.
					\item[NRSV] Alas for you, O land, when your king is a servant, and your princes feast in the morning!
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 10:17} % *ECCLESIASTES-10-17
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-10-17}

			Blessed art thou, O land, when thy king is the son of nobles, and thy princes eat in due season, for strength, and not for drunkenness!

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַשְׁרֵ֣יךְ אֶ֔רֶץ שֶׁמַּלְכֵּ֖ךְ בֶּן־חוֹרִ֑ים וְשָׂרַ֙יִךְ֙ בָּעֵ֣ת יֹאכֵ֔לוּ בִּגְבוּרָ֖ה וְלֹ֥א בַשְּׁתִֽי׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{μακαρία, γῆ, ἧς ὁ βασιλεύς σου υἱὸς ἐλευθέρων, καὶ οἱ ἄρχοντές σου πρὸς καιρὸν φάγονται ἐν δυνάμει καὶ οὐκ αἰσχυνθήσονται.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Happy art thou, O land, when thy king is the son of nobles, and thy princes eat in due season, for strength, and not for drunkenness!
					\item[NIV] Blessed is the land whose king is of noble birth and whose princes eat at a proper time— for strength and not for drunkenness.
					\item[NRSV] Happy are you, O land, when your king is a nobleman, and your princes feast at the proper time— for strength, and not for drunkenness!
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 10:18} % *ECCLESIASTES-10-18
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-10-18}

			By much slothfulness the building decayeth; and through idleness of the hands the house droppeth through.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בַּעֲצַלְתַּ֖יִם יִמַּ֣ךְ הַמְּקָרֶ֑ה וּבְשִׁפְל֥וּת יָדַ֖יִם יִדְלֹ֥ף הַבָּֽיִת׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{ἐν ὀκνηρίαις ταπεινωθήσεται ἡ δόκωσις, καὶ ἐν ἀργείᾳ χειρῶν στάξει ἡ οἰκία.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] By slothfulness the roof sinketh in; and through idleness of the hands the house leaketh.
					\item[NIV] Through laziness, the rafters sag; because of idle hands, the house leaks.
					\item[NRSV] Through sloth the roof sinks in, and through indolence the house leaks.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 10:19} % *ECCLESIASTES-10-19
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-10-19}

			A feast is made for laughter, and wine maketh merry: but money answereth all things.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לִשְׂחוֹק֙ עֹשִׂ֣ים לֶ֔חֶם וְיַ֖יִן יְשַׂמַּ֣ח חַיִּ֑ים וְהַכֶּ֖סֶף יַעֲנֶ֥ה אֶת־הַכֹּֽל׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{εἰς γέλωτα ποιοῦσιν ἄρτον, καὶ οἶνον καὶ ἔλαιον τοῦ εὐφρανθῆναι ζῶντας, καὶ τοῦ ἀργυρίου ταπεινώσει ἐπακούσεται τὰ πάντα.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A feast is made for laughter, and wine maketh glad the life; and money answereth all things.
					\item[NIV] A feast is made for laughter, wine makes life merry, and money is the answer for everything.
					\item[NRSV] Feasts are made for laughter; wine gladdens life, and money meets every need.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 10:20} % *ECCLESIASTES-10-20
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-10-20}

			Curse not the king, no not in thy thought; and curse not the rich in thy bedchamber: for a bird of the air shall carry the voice, and that which hath wings shall tell the matter.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גַּ֣ם בְּמַדָּֽעֲךָ֗ מֶ֚לֶךְ אַל־תְּקַלֵּ֔ל וּבְחַדְרֵי֙ מִשְׁכָּ֣בְךָ֔ אַל־תְּקַלֵּ֖ל עָשִׁ֑יר כִּ֣י ע֤וֹף הַשָּׁמַ֙יִם֙ יוֹלִ֣יךְ אֶת־הַקּ֔וֹל וּבַ֥עַל כְּנָפַ֖יִם יַגֵּ֥יד דָּבָֽר׃}
					\item[LXX] \gr{καί γε ἐν συνειδήσει σου βασιλέα μὴ καταράσῃ, καὶ ἐν ταμείοις κοιτώνων σου μὴ καταράσῃ πλούσιον· ὅτι πετεινὸν τοῦ οὐρανοῦ ἀποίσει σου τὴν φωνήν, καὶ ὁ τὰς πτέρυγας ἀπαγγελεῖ λόγον σου.}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Revile not the king, no, not in thy thought; and revile not the rich in thy bedchamber: for a bird of the heavens shall carry the voice, and that which hath wings shall tell the matter.
					\item[NIV] Do not revile the king even in your thoughts, or curse the rich in your bedroom, because a bird in the sky may carry your words, and a bird on the wing may report what you say.
					\item[NRSV] Do not curse the king, even in your thoughts, or curse the rich, even in your bedroom; for a bird of the air may carry your voice, or some winged creature tell the matter.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Ecclesiastes 11} % *ECCLESIASTES-11
	% -----
		\label{ECCLESIASTES-11}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Ecclesiastes 11:1} % *ECCLESIASTES-11-1
		% % ---
			\label{ECCLESIASTES-11-1}

			Cast thy bread upon the waters: for thou shalt find it after many days.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שַׁלַּ֥ח לַחְמְךָ֖ עַל־פְּנֵ֣י הַמָּ֑יִם כִּֽי־בְרֹ֥ב הַיָּמִ֖ים תִּמְצָאֶֽנּוּ׃}
